Once I can actually get myself to focus, finishing the tasks isn't the main issue for me.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself avoiding, procrastinating on, or strongly disliking tasks that require a lot of mental effort over a long period, like reading long documents or writing reports?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't really noticed myself avoiding or procrastinating on those kinds of tasks. I don't mind starting them, my main issue is just keeping my focus once I do.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find that your mind wanders when people are speaking directly to you, making it seem like you aren't listening?\n\nPatient: Yes, that happens to me all the time. Subtypes: Persistent Fear, Persistent Horror, Persistent Anger, Persistent Guilt, Persistent Shame\n[S075] Social_Detachment: Feelings of detachment or estrangement from others.
